BudgetAir.co.uk</h1><p>At BudgetAir.co.uk you can compare <a href="/airlines">800+ airlines</a> reaching <a href="/flights">9000+ destinations</a> worldwide. Thinking about a holiday in the Philippines, want to visit the city of Bangkok or looking for a cultural trip around Europe? You've come to the right place. We will help you find the great flight deals around the globe. Whether at home, work or in the pub, our website is user friendly on all devices. Book your airline tickets right away, wherever you are!</p><h2>How do you find the cheap flights?</h2><ul><li>There could be cheap deals for flights at any time! A good tip is to search for flights on Tuesday, and to book flights that fly on Wednesday.</li><li>It’s usually better to buy your tickets 4-5 months in advance.</li><li>In a mood to steal a deal? Check out our <a href="/discount">promotion page</a> with exclusive deals from airlines and active vouchers codes.</li><li><a href="/last-minutes">Last Minute Deals</a> can also save you money. But you need a bit of luck as the price can vary due to the season and availability.</li><li>Flying there and back with different airlines or from different <a href="/airports">airports</a> may help as well keeping the trip budget low. Use our filters during the search process and find the ticket that best suits your needs.</li></ul><h2>Why are prices of airline tickets different?</h2><p>Flight ticket cost depends on several factors. First, the airline determines how much an airline ticket costs. This price differs by booking class. If you are looking to book a flight months in advance, ticket prices are generally lower. In the week before the flight departs, the prices of airline tickets for this flight will go down or just up, depending on the purpose of the airline and market demand. Other factors affecting airline prices include changes in fuel costs, airport tax and maintenance costs.</p><h2>What name do I have to fill in on the airline ticket?</h2><p>The name you have to fill in on your ticket must be the same as the name in your passport. That means: the first name in the passport and the last name. You do not have to enter your middle name. Pay attention to the name you write down when you are making your booking as changing a name later can cost a pretty penny! If your name is not correct on your e-ticket, we recommend that you report this to us as quickly as possible to minimise costs.</p><h2>How do I book flights with multiple destinations?</h2><p>Do you want to combine a trip to Barcelona with a visit to Madrid? Good idea! You can put together your own itinerary by combining several <a href="/flights">flights</a> in one booking. Choose the option 'Multiple destinations' in our search box. You can fly back from a different airport than the one you arrived at.</p><h2>Need some inspiration?</h2><p>Starving to hit the road but have no clue where to go? No panic, we can help! Check our <strong>Trips For Any Taste</strong> lists with the all sorts of the holidays destinations: <a href="/citybreaks">city break</a>, <a href="/beach-holidays">beach vacation</a> or <a href="/family">family holiday</a>. Now it's up to you which one you pick! Whether you want to <a href="/citybreaks/fly-and-eat">fly and eat</a>, <a href="/citybreaks/fly-and-shop">fly and shop</a> or <a href="/citybreaks/fly-and-discover">fly and discover</a>, we got you covered!</p></div></div></div></div></div></section><section class="newsletter-and-social"><header class="main-header align-center ink-grid"><h2>For support and inspiration</h2></header><div
